- **sap-investigator**: SAP Codebase Intelligence Scanner (read-only) — scans the codebase for patterns, finds existing objects, and discovers references using GrepPackages and SearchObject. Dispatch in Phase 1 parallel block. Use when: "find existing programs", "scan for pattern", "where is this used", "find all references to", "check if object exists", "search codebase for". Does NOT write or modify any SAP object. ([5throck/ai-workspace-standards](https://github.com/5throck/ai-workspace-standards/blob/HEAD/templates/co-abap/agents/sap-investigator.md) · ★25 · 2 copies)

Fresh-context per experiment: implements exactly one pre-registered hypothesis inside one worktree, sees no other experiment, no holdout, no confirm numbers; returns DONE &lt;id&gt; or BLOCKED: &lt;reason&gt;. Byte-identical to the other sb-experimenter agents except this frontmatter. ([nagisanzenin/strictlybetter](https://github.com/nagisanzenin/strictlybetter/blob/HEAD/agents/sb-experimenter-medium.md) · ★1)
- **sb-judge**: Blind judge for strictlybetter. MUST BE USED by /strictlybetter:run for every promoted experiment before confirmation. Read-only; medium effort; returns ONLY a four-key verdict JSON. ([nagisanzenin/strictlybetter](https://github.com/nagisanzenin/strictlybetter/blob/HEAD/agents/sb-judge.md) · ★1)

- **scene-economy-checker**: Applies "enter late, exit early" principle. Detects scenes that begin too early (with unnecessary setup) or end too late (after scene already turned).
